Reporting Central User Guide
FR 2436
Semiannual Report of Derivatives Activity
Federal Reserve System
Statistics Function October 2026
2 Overview
The Federal Reserve System’s Reporting Central Application provides the ability to submit spreadsheet files, via the internet. As a result, reporting institutions that use a spreadsheet to prepare their data will not have to key their data into a web page form. While this feature was provided for reporting institutions using spreadsheets, the file can be created by any other backend system that a reporting institution uses to generate its data.
This document provides the required file format for submitting the data via Reporting Central and demonstrates how a reporting institution can create the file from a spreadsheet.
We used Excel for our examples but the same process can be applied to other spreadsheet software. The method utilized to create the file is at the discretion of the reporter.
This guide assumes that your data already exist in a spreadsheet.
This document demonstrates how to create the file for the FR 2436 report. The report name for Reporting Central will be FR 2436. We use formulae to pull the data from the spreadsheet worksheets containing the data and format them for Reporting Central.
3
Example: FR 2436
The spreadsheet example created for the FR 2436 contains two Excel worksheets (Sheet 1, Sheet 2).
• Sheet 1 contains the report data created from the respondent’s backend system or from a spreadsheet template.
• Sheet 2 contains a set of formulas that pulls data from Sheet 1 and creates strings for the report header record, rows of data, options remarks field, end-of text indicator, and each edit explanation.
Step 1) Assemble report data in Sheet1:
• Respondent ID
• Series Name
• Report As-of Date
• A row of data for each line of the report
Figure 1: FR 2436 “Sheet 1” (Data View)
Note: In this example the respondent has a spreadsheet with a layout like the FR 2436 report form. It includes all data needed for the report header line, each report data line, and optional comment columns that are not transmitted to Reporting Central but make the meaning of the content clearer for the preparer.
4 Step 2) Create formulas to pull in data from Sheet1 to create the text strings for the report header, each line of data, the remarks field, and edit explanations
Figure 2: FR 2436 “Sheet 2” (Financial Data Items)
NOTE: In this example the respondent adds a worksheet (Sheet2) containing formulas to pull data from Sheet1 and create text strings that will be combined into the format required by Reporting Central in the next step.
5 Sheet 2 Formulas and Strings
Report Header Record Excel formula used:
Header: =Sheet1!B2&Sheet1!B3&" "&Sheet1!B4
Text string created by formula: =Sheet1!A5&"+"&Sheet1!B5
Explanation of Excel formula: The formula begins with an equal sign (=) and then combines the respondent ID and series name (FR2436) with the cell reference Sheet1!B4 containing the as-of date to create the string with the header information. Since the series name is a 10-character field and FR2436 occupies only 6 spaces, 4 additional spaces have been added in between the contents of cells B3 and B4 using quotation marks.
FR2436 Table 1A, Line”Currency code”, column “TFXC” Excel formula used: =Sheet1!A5&"+"&Sheet1!B5 Text string created by formula: LDERVT147+ Explanation of Excel formula: The formula begins with an equal sign (=) and then combines the line number “LDERVT147” (Cell A5) with Cell B5 (Currency code for total foreign exchange contracts) to create the string of data for the line and column of the report.
The Excel formula connects the contents of the cells together into a string with an ampersand (&). The file uses a plus sign (+) between the line number and the data item of each row.
FR2436 remainder of Table 1A items, tables 1B through 6 Use the formula, string, and explanation for the example above as your guide for creating the remaining formulas
Step 3) Save the file as a text file.
While in Sheet2, use the following steps to save the file:
• Choose File, Save As... to open the Save As window
• Enter the name of the file you want to create into the File name box
• Select Text (Tab Delimited)(*.txt) in the Save as type: box
• Click on the Save button to save the file
• Click on the OK button to acknowledge that you want "To save only the active sheet" (Sheet2)
6
Step 4) Submit the file to the Federal Reserve using the Reporting Central system. Steps are below.
7 Report Submission in Reporting Central Cloud In Reporting Central, select the “Upload file” tab and then click the “Upload your files here” tab.
Then, navigate to the stored text file and select the filename so it appears in the ‘File name:’ box. Select “Open” to continue and then hit the “Upload” button.
If there are no formatting issues during the file upload you will get a “File Upload Complete” message the data will load directly into the report screen.
The report data can then be validated, saved, submitted or deleted. If errors exist in the text file error messages will appear in the file upload window, which can be used to troubleshoot any formatting issues.
APPENDIX A
8
File Format for FR 2436 (Effective 06/30/2016)
Report Header Record for FR 2436 The Report Header Record must be the first record in the file.
Report Data Record for FR 2436 There must be one Report Data Record for each line of the report form.
ml
Note: Do not zero fill the report. If you do not have data for a cell, leave the cell null. The value of certain cells (shaded cells) of data must be null [e.g., Line 1 currency code in Table 1A to 2C, (columns 1 to 16, and column 32), must be null].
